TOTAL            178,942   3,980    2,804     2,663          1,646 | 0.0007 0.0202 0.0445 16.016  0.0002 0.0045 0.0038 0.0221 1,646 | 0.92% 2.22% 1.57% 1.49% fzc

Mode	Meaning
0	Raw bytes (no transform).
1	Delta encoding (successive-byte differences).
2	XOR-adjacent.
3	Nibble swap.
4	Bitwise invert.
5	BMP row-horizontal delta (BI_RGB-style payload).
6	Nested gzip (bit-exact restore): stores original gzip length + SHA-1 + inner mode + inner payload; decode expands inner then restores exact original gzip bytes (pairs with FZG* trailers on the container when used from zip_folder).
7	Single-member ZIP (semantic): inner file name + inner mode + payload; decode rebuilds a ZIP with that one member.
8	Single-file 7z (semantic): inner path + inner mode + payload; decode rebuilds a 7z with that file.
9	Raster semantic: on-disk bytes + FZRC1 canonical tail; decode returns disk bytes (lossless identity for formats where “canonical” differs).
10	Raster dedup: disk bytes + index into an FZBD canonical table (from the container).
11	MPQ / MoPAQ semantic: tag + inner mode + payload; decode rebuilds MPQ via the Python semantic pack path.
12	Full-buffer byte reverse (strrev).
13	BMP column-vertical delta.
14	Square-block transpose on a leading s×s prefix; rest unchanged.
15	BMP uniform grid (BSS1 + per-cell scalar).
16	BMP grid core + rim (BSS2 + per-cell shifts + raw rims).
17	Chained transforms: sequence of modes applied in order (nested “mini-program”).
